2026-10-25T06:26:48.4620859Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4622024Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4622650Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4622934Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4623650Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4624111Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4624333Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4625052Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4625688Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4625987Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4626467Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4627088Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4627598Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628054Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628184Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628346Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628602Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628754Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4629073Z         return bool(match)

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4632565Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4660136Z def write_x_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, switch_options, x_conf_dir_path, root):

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4662340Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4662967Z     :param switch_options: list of options for layout switching
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4663303Z     :type switch_options: list(str)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4663660Z     :param x_conf_dir_path: path to directory holding X Layouts configuration
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4664036Z     :type x_conf_dir_path: str
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4664306Z     :param root: root path of the configured system
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4664594Z     :type root: str
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4664783Z     """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4664880Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4664961Z     errors = []
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4665149Z     try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4665351Z         if not os.path.isdir(x_conf_dir_path):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4665644Z             os.makedirs(x_conf_dir_path)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4665902Z     except OSError:

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4666689Z         log.debug("Cannot create X Layouts configuration directory %s", x_conf_dir_path)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4667014Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4667089Z     if root != "/":
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4667384Z         # Writing to a different root, we need to save these values, so that
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4667797Z         # we can restore them when we have the file written out.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4668201Z         saved_layouts_variants = localed_wrapper.get_layouts_variants()
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4668582Z         saved_options = localed_wrapper.options
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4668784Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4668960Z     # Set systemd-localed's layouts, variants and switch options, which
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4669319Z     # also generates a new conf file.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4669634Z     localed_wrapper.set_layouts(x_layouts, switch_options)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4670050Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4670133Z     if root != "/":
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4670403Z         # Make sure the target directory exists under the given root.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4670926Z         rooted_xconf_dir = os.path.normpath(root + "/" + x_conf_dir_path)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4671272Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4671928Z             if not os.path.isdir(rooted_xconf_dir):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4672504Z                 os.makedirs(rooted_xconf_dir)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4673005Z         except OSError:

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4674418Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4674597Z         # Copy the file to the chroot.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4675266Z         xconf_file_path = os.path.normpath(x_conf_dir_path + "/" + X_CONF_FILE_NAME)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4676116Z         rooted_xconf_file_path = os.path.normpath(root + "/" + xconf_file_path)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4676812Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4677269Z             shutil.copy2(xconf_file_path, rooted_xconf_file_path)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4677849Z         except OSError as ioerr:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4678634Z             log.error("Cannot copy X layouts configuration file %s to target system: %s.",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4679434Z                       xconf_file_path, ioerr.strerror)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4679815Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4679985Z         # Restore the original values.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4680642Z         localed_wrapper.set_layouts(saved_layouts_variants, saved_options)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4681162Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4681301Z     if errors:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4683260Z         raise KeyboardInstallationError("\n".join(errors))

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4683914Z def write_vc_configuration(vc_keymap, root):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4684535Z     """Write virtual console keyboard mapping configuration.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4684983Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4685262Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4685833Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4686289Z     :param root: root path of the configured system
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4686792Z     :type root: str
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4687116Z     """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4687410Z     try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4687814Z         fpath = os.path.normpath(root + VC_CONF_FILE_PATH)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4688206Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4688494Z         # "eurlatgr" may not be the best choice for all the languages,
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4689212Z         # for example, Russian, Urdu, Serbian, Sindhi and a few more.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4689932Z         # refer: https://bugzilla.redhat.com/show_bug.cgi?id=1919486
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4690540Z         # assuming that LANG is set by now.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4691117Z         vc_fonts = get_locale_console_fonts(os.environ["LANG"])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4692892Z         vc_font = vc_fonts[0] if vc_fonts else DEFAULT_VC_FONT
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4693310Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4693495Z         with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4693991Z             fobj.write('KEYMAP="%s"\n' % vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4694352Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4694648Z             # systemd now defaults to a font that cannot display non-ascii

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4824861Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4825557Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4826010Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4826412Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4827133Z     """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4827548Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4827689Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4828060Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4828306Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4828477Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4828953Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4829419Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4829625Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4830222Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4830626Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4830815Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4831873Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4832460Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4832811Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4833631Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4834563Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4835480Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4836333Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4837329Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4838119Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4838683Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4839360Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4840017Z             #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4840876Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4841895Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4842695Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4843449Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4844364Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4844990Z             #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4845616Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4846343Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4846641Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4847466Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4848258Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4848947Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4849482Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4849703Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4850261Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4850769Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4851300Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4852381Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4852991Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4853514Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4854108Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4854789Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4855556Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4856224Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4856808Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4857497Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4858461Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4859001Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4859234Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4859629Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4859777Z     @property
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4860330Z     def current_layout_variant(self):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4860868Z         """Get first (current) layout with variant.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4861237Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4861939Z         :return: a list of "layout (variant)" or "layout" layout specifications
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4862601Z         :rtype: list(str)

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4863442Z         layouts_variants = self.get_layouts_variants()
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4864077Z         return "" if not layouts_variants else layouts_variants[0]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4864513Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4868811Z         return self._last_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4869137Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4869461Z     def set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4870099Z         """Set X11 layouts.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4870345Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4875392Z                         (see set_and_convert_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4875880Z         :type convert: bool
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4876257Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4876682Z         self._set_layouts(layouts_variants, options, convert)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4877384Z         log.debug("Storing layouts for compositor configured by user")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4878060Z         self._user_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4878438Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4878759Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4879435Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4879770Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4880066Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4880642Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4881224Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4882400Z         if options is None:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4882805Z             options = self.options
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4883231Z             log.debug(
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4883885Z                 "Keyboard layouts for compositor are missing options. Use compositor options: %s",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4884637Z                 options,
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4884986Z             )
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4885176Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4885588Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4886595Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4887524Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4887845Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4888478Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4889235Z             options,
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4889580Z         )
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4890173Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4890478Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4890580Z         # store configuration from user
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4890918Z         super().set_layouts(layouts_variants, options, convert)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4891163Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4891274Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4892214Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4892632Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4892997Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4893852Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4894334Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4894686Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4895350Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4895775Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4896247Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4896743Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4897094Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4897571Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4898217Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4898559Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4898684Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4899120Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4899705Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4900162Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4900535Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4901101Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4902116Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4902605Z             return False
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4902867Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4903007Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4903471Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4904502Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4905078Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4905464Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4906163Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4906433Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4906634Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4907132Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4907512Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4907856Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4907999Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4908143Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4908409Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4908605Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4908974Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4909210Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4909655Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910062Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910195Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910287Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910584Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910812Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4911047Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4912108Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4912872Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4913541Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4913769Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4913949Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4914423Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4914728Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915038Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915235Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915450Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915734Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915861Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915962Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4916387Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4916809Z             return False
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4916948Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4917032Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4917310Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4917626Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4917874Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4918152Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4918369Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4918446Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4918711Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4919105Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4919442Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4919700Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4919920Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4920268Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4920654Z             return False

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5056955Z from pyanaconda.anaconda_loggers import get_module_logger
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5057258Z from pyanaconda.core.constants import DEFAULT_KEYBOARD

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5057900Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5057905Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058297Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058517Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058524Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058687Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058936Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5059101Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5059424Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5059591Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5059804Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5060130Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5060486Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5060696Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5060705Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5061047Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5061236Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5062414Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5062582Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5062839Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5062979Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5063229Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5063642Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5063774Z     """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5063942Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5064389Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5064754Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5064765Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5065035Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5065514Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5065699Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5066031Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5066271Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067088Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067752Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067762Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067930Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5068715Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5069416Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5069672Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5070225Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5112113Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5112482Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5112840Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5113183Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5113310Z #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5113439Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5113798Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5114188Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5114447Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5114798Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5114923Z #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115050Z set -eu
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115057Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115197Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115327Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115489Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115622Z do
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115762Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115895Z         in
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116055Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116237Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116493Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116629Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116773Z     esac
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116898Z done
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116906Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5117402Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5117414Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5117587Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5117750Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5117880Z then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5118031Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5118164Z fi

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5118698Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5118788Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5118974Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119099Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119169Z     #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119375Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119478Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119623Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119739Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119809Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120015Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120291Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120296Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120473Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120577Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120832Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120908Z       fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120977Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120981Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5121097Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5121656Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5121669Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5121946Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122242Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122440Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122530Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122635Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122705Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122847Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122917Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122926Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123057Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123151Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123218Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123222Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123307Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123596Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123796Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123876Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123951Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123955Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124080Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124188Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124360Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124438Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124510Z         fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124587Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124682Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124753Z     done
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124760Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125019Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125257Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125364Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125546Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125771Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125866Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125936Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126112Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126195Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126263Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126335Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126339Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126423Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126507Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126514Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126730Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126869Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127060Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127064Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127191Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127291Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127365Z     then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127547Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127644Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127715Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127891Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127895Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5128063Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5128384Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5128501Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5128506Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5128720Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5128823Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5128917Z     # set environment for Firefox
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129190Z     BROWSER_ENV=(MOZ_APP_TITLE="" MOZ_APP_REMOTINGNAME="liveinst" MOZ_GTK_TITLEBAR_DECORATION=client)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129194Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129499Z     BROWSER=(/usr/bin/env "${BROWSER_ENV[@]}" /usr/bin/firefox --new-instance --profile "${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129508Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129590Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129663Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129667Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129670Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129756Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129830Z then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5129919Z     /bin/sleep infinity &
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130000Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130073Z else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130171Z     USER_INFO=$(setup_user_environment)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130305Z     INSTALLER_USER=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f1)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130422Z     HOME_DIR=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f2)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130429Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130562Z     # if we have netcat, use it for waiting until ws is up
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130656Z     if type nc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130738Z         for _ in `seq 10`; do
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130843Z             nc -z "$WEBUI_ADDRESS" 80 && break
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130928Z             sleep 0.5;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5130999Z         done
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5131073Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5131161Z         # otherwise, just wait a bit
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5131238Z         sleep 3
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5131307Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5131316Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5133444Z     readarray -t user_environment < <(pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" env XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}" systemctl --user show-environment)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5133471Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5135455Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5136472Z     HOME="$BROWSER_HOME" XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP=GNOME \
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5137149Z     pkexec --user $INSTALLER_USER env DISPLAY=$DISPLAY "${user_environment[@]}" "${BROWSER[@]}" http://"$WEBUI_ADDRESS""$URL_PATH" &
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5137314Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5150883Z action() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151024Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151030Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151160Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151330Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151757Z     shift
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151898Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152074Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152219Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152376Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152503Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152627Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152765Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152890Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5153024Z }

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5154218Z RES_COL=60

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5154566Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5154849Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155030Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155309Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155471Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155757Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155927Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156142Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156308Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156315Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156475Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156763Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156987Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156994Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157140Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157285Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157430Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157569Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157706Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157855Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158000Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158138Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158279Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158402Z         ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158532Z esac
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158538Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158697Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158825Z success() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159010Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159136Z     return 0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159260Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159266Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159425Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159551Z failure() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159683Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159855Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160065Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160197Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160492Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160498Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160636Z echo_success() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160803Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160944Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5161133Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5161654Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5161869Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162008Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162147Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162275Z     return 0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162400Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162407Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162546Z echo_failure() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162710Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162847Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163025Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_FAILURE
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163189Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163366Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163508Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163646Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163769Z     return 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163892Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163898Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164030Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164171Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164310Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164443Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164603Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164742Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164867Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165007Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165180Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165313Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165455Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165591Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165761Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165883Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166065Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166277Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166520Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166648Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166770Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166937Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167112Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167237Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167368Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167531Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167697Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167817Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167949Z         *)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5168233Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5168392Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5168519Z     esac
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5168638Z }

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5206505Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5206649Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5206833Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207025Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207213Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207342Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207348Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207607Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207803Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5208139Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5208294Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5208461Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5208717Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209087Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209219Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209409Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209540Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209786Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209803Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209990Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5210164Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5210292Z     echo
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5210483Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5210884Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5211060Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5211191Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5212720Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213050Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213170Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213179Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213337Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213558Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213838Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214148Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214375Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214586Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214769Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214896Z }

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5215371Z   homedev=${cut%% *}

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4605166Z         :param sysroot: a path to the root of the installed system

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4610522Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4613955Z     def _get_supported_language(self):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4614618Z         """Return a supported locale.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4615096Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4615380Z         :return: a locale
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4615886Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4616470Z         if self._is_language_support_installed(self._lang):

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4620473Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4620855Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4622013Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4622456Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4622931Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4623646Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4624107Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4624330Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4625048Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4625685Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4625984Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4626463Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4627085Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4627594Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628051Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628182Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628344Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628600Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4628747Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4629071Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4629221Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4629344Z     def _write_language_configuration(self, lang):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4629709Z         """Write language configuration to the /etc/locale.conf file.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4629970Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4630103Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4630384Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4630558Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4630827Z             fpath = join_paths(self._sysroot, self.LOCALE_CONF_FILE_PATH)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4631235Z             log.debug("Writing the '%s' locale to %s.", lang, fpath)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4631870Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4632054Z             with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4632561Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4632917Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4633072Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4633703Z             msg = "Cannot write language configuration file: {}".format(e.strerror)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4634459Z             raise LanguageInstallationError(msg) from e

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4687804Z         fpath = os.path.normpath(root + VC_CONF_FILE_PATH)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4688202Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4688490Z         # "eurlatgr" may not be the best choice for all the languages,

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4695983Z             fobj.write('FONT="%s"\n' % vc_font)

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4824857Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4825551Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4826005Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4826409Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4827129Z     """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4827543Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4828303Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4829622Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4830623Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4830812Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4831867Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4832450Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4832807Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4833625Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4834557Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4835475Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4836330Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4837318Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4838116Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4838678Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4839352Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4840010Z             #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4840867Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4841885Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4842689Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4843444Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4844354Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4844985Z             #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4845599Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4846337Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4846637Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4847459Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4848252Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4848941Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4849476Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4849698Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4850256Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4850764Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4851295Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4852373Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4852976Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4853509Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4854105Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4854786Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4855552Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4856220Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4856804Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4857493Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4858455Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4858993Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4859229Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4859622Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4878756Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4879425Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4879768Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4880062Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4880639Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4881214Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4882392Z         if options is None:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4882801Z             options = self.options
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4883227Z             log.debug(

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4884633Z                 options,
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4884983Z             )
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4885172Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4885585Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4886592Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4887520Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4887842Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4888474Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4889230Z             options,
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4889575Z         )
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4889994Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4890475Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4890578Z         # store configuration from user

2026-10-25T06:26:48.4891161Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4891272Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4892205Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4892628Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4892993Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4893848Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4894330Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4894683Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4895346Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4895771Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4896237Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4896738Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4897089Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4897565Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4898213Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4898555Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4898680Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4899116Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4899701Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4900158Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4900532Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4901098Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4902109Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4902600Z             return False
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4902862Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4903002Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4903454Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4904491Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4905072Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4905460Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4906159Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4906430Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4906631Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4907128Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4907509Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4907854Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4907991Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4908140Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4908407Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4908603Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4908972Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4909208Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4909649Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910059Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910193Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910285Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910582Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4910811Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4911045Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4912101Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4912869Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4913538Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4913767Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4913947Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4914317Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4914726Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915036Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915233Z         """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915448Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915732Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915859Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4915960Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4916384Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4916807Z             return False
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4916946Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4917030Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4917308Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4917618Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4917872Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4918150Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4918363Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4918444Z         try:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4918708Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4919103Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4919440Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4919698Z             return True
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4919918Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4920266Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.4920652Z             return False

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5057903Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058293Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058513Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058522Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058684Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5058933Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5059096Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5059418Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5059588Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5059799Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5060124Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5060471Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5060691Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5060702Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5061044Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5061231Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5062404Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5062579Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5062835Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5062976Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5063224Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5063635Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5063770Z     """
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5063938Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5064384Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5064570Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5064761Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5065032Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5065509Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5065694Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5066026Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5066266Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5066276Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5066449Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5066898Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067077Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067085Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067250Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067739Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067759Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067926Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067934Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5067938Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5068295Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5068448Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5068709Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5069111Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5069275Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5069412Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5069669Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5070065Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5070222Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5070229Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5070391Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5112107Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5112478Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5112837Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5113180Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5113307Z #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5113436Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5113794Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5114176Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5114444Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5114795Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5114921Z #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115047Z set -eu
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115054Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115324Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115619Z do
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115758Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5115891Z         in
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116051Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116233Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116490Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116626Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116769Z     esac
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116895Z done
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5116903Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5117382Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5117411Z

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5118695Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5118786Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5118972Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119097Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119167Z     #
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119372Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119471Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119621Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119737Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5119808Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120013Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120288Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120294Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120471Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120575Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120829Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120906Z       fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120975Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5120979Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5121095Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5121260Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5121666Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5121942Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122238Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122433Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122528Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122633Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122703Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122845Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122915Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5122925Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123055Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123150Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123216Z }

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123305Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123590Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123790Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123874Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123949Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5123953Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124078Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124186Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124358Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124437Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124508Z         fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124585Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124676Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124751Z     done
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5124759Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125016Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125255Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125358Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125544Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125769Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125864Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5125935Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126110Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126192Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126262Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126333Z }

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126421Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126506Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126513Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126728Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5126867Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127054Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127063Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5127189Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5147792Z #!/bin/sh
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5148060Z # Functions to be used with livesys-* scripts
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5148286Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5148740Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5148943Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5149324Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5149494Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5149629Z fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5149833Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5150131Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5150311Z strstr() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5150453Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5150577Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5150584Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5150754Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5150881Z action() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151021Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151028Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151156Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151326Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151751Z     shift
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5151894Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152070Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152216Z     else
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152373Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152500Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152624Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152762Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5152887Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5153014Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5153028Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5153316Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5153475Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5153784Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5153917Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5154085Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5154215Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5154394Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5154563Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5154845Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155027Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155306Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155468Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155753Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5155916Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156139Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156305Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156312Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156472Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156760Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156980Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5156992Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157135Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157282Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157427Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157567Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157703Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157852Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5157998Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158135Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158276Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158399Z         ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158523Z esac
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158536Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158694Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5158822Z success() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159006Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159133Z     return 0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159256Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159264Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159422Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159548Z failure() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159680Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5159852Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160062Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160194Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160488Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160496Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160633Z echo_success() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160799Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5160941Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5161130Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5161287Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5161864Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162005Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162143Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162272Z     return 0
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162397Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162404Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162543Z echo_failure() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162706Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5162844Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163022Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_FAILURE
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163186Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163363Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163505Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163643Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163766Z     return 1
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163889Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5163896Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164027Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164168Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164301Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164440Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164600Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164739Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5164864Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165003Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165177Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165310Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165441Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165588Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165758Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5165880Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166062Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166273Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166517Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166645Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166767Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5166934Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167095Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167235Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167366Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167528Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167694Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167814Z             ;;
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5167946Z         *)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5168229Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5168388Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5168516Z     esac
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5168635Z }

2026-10-25T06:26:48.5206502Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5206646Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5206830Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207021Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207210Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207338Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207346Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207604Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5207794Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5208136Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5208290Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5208458Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5208714Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209082Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209216Z     fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209404Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209538Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209781Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209800Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5209985Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5210161Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5210289Z     echo
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5210479Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5210870Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5211056Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5211188Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5212705Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213046Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213167Z   fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213175Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213334Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213554Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5213835Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214145Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214372Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214583Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214766Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214886Z }
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5214900Z
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5215074Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5215227Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5215368Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5215632Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5215848Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5215975Z fi
2026-10-25T06:26:48.5215982Z
